  $wsimpleTable ::
    [Blocks]
    -> [[Blocks]]
    -> (# Data.Sequence.Internal.Elem Text.Pandoc.Definition.Block #)
  [TagSig: <TagTuple[TagDunno]>, LambdaFormInfo: LFReEntrant 2,
   Arity: 2, Strictness: <L><L>, Inline: [2],
   Unfolding: Core: <vanilla>
              \ (headers['GHC.Types.Many] :: [Blocks])
                (rows['GHC.Types.Many] :: [[Blocks]]) ->
              $wtableWith
                Text.Pandoc.Definition.nullAttr
                emptyCaption
                (case GHC.Internal.Base.map
                        @[Blocks]
                        @GHC.Types.Int
                        (GHC.Internal.List.length @Blocks)
                        (GHC.Types.: @[Blocks] headers rows) of wild {
                   []
                   -> case GHC.Internal.List.maximum2
                      ret_ty [Text.Pandoc.Definition.ColSpec]
                      of {}
                   : ipv ipv1
                   -> case ipv of wild1 { GHC.Types.I# ww ->
                      case $w$wgo3 ipv1 ww of ww1 { DEFAULT ->
                      case GHC.Prim.<# 0# ww1 of lwild {
                        DEFAULT -> GHC.Types.[] @Text.Pandoc.Definition.ColSpec
                        1# -> $wxs1 ww1 } } } })
                (case headers of wild {
                   [] -> simpleTable3
                   : ds1 ds2
                   -> Text.Pandoc.Definition.TableHead
                        Text.Pandoc.Definition.nullAttr
                        (GHC.Types.:
                           @Text.Pandoc.Definition.Row
                           (case GHC.Internal.Base.map
                                   @(Many Text.Pandoc.Definition.Block)
                                   @Text.Pandoc.Definition.Cell
                                   simpleCell
                                   wild of conrep { DEFAULT ->
                            Text.Pandoc.Definition.Row
                              Text.Pandoc.Definition.nullAttr
                              conrep })
                           (GHC.Types.[] @Text.Pandoc.Definition.Row)) })

                   @Text.Pandoc.Definition.TableBody

                    Text.Pandoc.Definition.TableBody

                      0#

                      conrep })
                   (GHC.Types.[] @Text.Pandoc.Definition.TableBody))
                simpleTable2]

              (# case attr of conrep { (,,) ipv ipv1 ipv2 ->
                 case capt of conrep1 { Text.Pandoc.Definition.Caption ipv3 ipv4 ->

                     GHC.Types.I# ww1 }

                        (case twidth of wild1 { GHC.Types.I# y ->
                         case GHC.Prim.<# 0# y of lwild {
                           DEFAULT -> GHC.Types.[] @Text.Pandoc.Definition.RowSpan

                 case GHC.Internal.Base.map
                        @Text.Pandoc.Definition.TableBody
                        @Text.Pandoc.Definition.TableBody

                        tbs of conrep4 { DEFAULT ->
                 case tf of wild1 { Text.Pandoc.Definition.TableFoot attr2 rows1 ->
                 case normalizeTableBody_normalizeRows'1
                        (case twidth of wild2 { GHC.Types.I# y ->
                         case GHC.Prim.<# 0# y of lwild {

  [TagSig: <TagProper>, LambdaFormInfo: LFReEntrant 2, Arity: 2,
